DSL</p><h3 id="introduction" class="scroll-m-20 h3">Introduction</h3><p>Last week</p><p>FireEye</p><p>shared that</p><p>they</p><p>experienced</p><p>unauthorized access and theft of their</p><p>offensive security</p><p>tools</p><p>used</p><p>by their red team</p><p>, by a sophisticated state-sponsored adversary</p><p>.</p><p>Although the theft of these sophisticated tools will</p><p>have</p><p>an impact on future attacks carried out by the adversary, how they accessed the tools was</p><p>a much bigger problem. Over the weekend FireEye</p><p>shared more details of their compromise and broke the news</p><p>that</p><p>they fell victim to a supply-chain attack involving</p><p>the</p><p>IT services</p><p>company SolarWinds</p><p>. FireEye reported the SolarWinds Orion software update had a backdoor injected into its code</p><p>,</p><p>which SolarWinds believed to have been included in updates</p><p>released between March and June 2020</p><p>. It should be noted,</p><p>h</p><p>owever</p><p>,</p><p>that</p><p>some researchers</p><p>have</p><p>report</p><p>ed</p><p>seeing activity as early as late 2019</p><p>.</p><p>The backdoor was dubbed SUNBURST by FireEye.</p><p>[hubspot type=cta portal=7924572 id=ec572148-ebc2-449f-8ccc-0353bc94df5e]</p><p>This supply-chain attack still</p><p>has an</p><p>unknown impact</p><p>. According to SolarWinds’ website their customer</p><p>s</p><p>include</p><p>many</p><p>U.S. Government agencies, as well as</p><p>a significant</p><p>percentage</p><p>of the Fortune 500.</p><p>According to</p><p>a recent filing</p><p>with the</p><p>US</p><p>Securities and Exchange Commission</p><p>(SEC)</p><p>it is</p><p>estimate</p><p>d that at least 18,000 installed the</p><p>malicious</p><p>update</p><p>.</p><p>Due to the nature of the attack, including the supply-chain compromise,</p><p>the</p><p>actor’s extreme attention to detail and operational security, as well as the high-profile targets who were successfully compromised as a result of the attack,</p><p>it is suspected that it was conducted by a nation-state sponsored group.</p><p>Volexity</p><p>researchers</p><p>have</p><p>attributed this</p><p>attack to a threat actor under the name of Dark Halo</p><p>, whom they have been tracking since late 2019</p><p>.</p><p>SUNBURST, the implant delivered via the backdoored SolarWinds Orion update,</p><p>was found in one of the DLL files contained in the update, specifically</p><p>“</p><p>SolarWinds</p><p>.Orion.Core.BusinessLayer.dll</p><p>”</p><p>.</p><p>According to</p><p>FireEye</p><p>, the backdoor has a dormant period of up to two weeks, and then will attempt to resolve a subdomain of</p><p>avsvmcloud</p><p>[</p><p>.</p><p>]com</p><p>, which</p><p>will return a</p><p>DNS</p><p>CNAME record</p><p>pointing</p><p>to a</p><p>c</p><p>ommand</p><p>and</p><p>c</p><p>ontrol</p><p>(C2)</p><p>domain. We go into detail on the functionality of SUNBURST</p><p>in our</p><p>Cyborg Labs Threat Hunt</p><p>Deep Dive</p><p>,</p><p>in which we review the SolarWinds supply-chain compromise, and examine the SUNBURST implant and how it behaves in an environment.</p><h3 id="update-17-december-2020" class="scroll-m-20 h3">UPDATE: 17 December 2020</h3><p>We are releasing 3 threat detection packages that will allow organizations to detect SUNBURST activity in their environment.</p><h3 id="threat-detection-packages" class="scroll-m-20 h3">Threat Detection Packages</h3><h3 id="sunburst-known-malicious-dns-activity" class="scroll-m-20 h3">SUNBURST Known Malicious DNS Activity</h3><h4 id="splunk" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Splunk</h4><p>(query_type IN (&quot;CNAME&quot;,&quot;A&quot;) AND (query=&quot;*avsvmcloud.com&quot; OR answer=&quot;*avsvmcloud.com&quot;)) OR (query_type=&quot;A&quot; AND query IN (&quot;deftsecurity.com&quot;,&quot;freescanonline.com&quot;, &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot;, &quot;websitetheme.com&quot;, &quot;highdatabase.com&quot;, &quot;incomeupdate.com&quot;, &quot;databasegalore.com&quot;, &quot;panhardware.com&quot;, &quot;zupertech.com&quot;, &quot;freescanonline.com&quot;, &quot;deftsecurity.com&quot;, &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot;))<br/>| stats values(_time) as occurrences count by src, query, query_type, answer<br/>| convert ctime(occurrences)</p><h4 id="elastic-lucene" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic Lucene</h4><p>((dns.question.name:&quot;/.*avsvmcloud\\.com/&quot; or dns.answer.name:&quot;/.*avsvmcloud\\.com/&quot;) and (dns.question.type:&quot;/[Cc][Nn][Aa][Mm][Ee]|[Aa]/&quot; or dns.answer.type:&quot;/[Cc][Nn][Aa][Mm][Ee]|[Aa]/&quot;)) or (dns.question.name:(&quot;deftsecurity.com&quot; or &quot;freescanonline.com&quot; or &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot; or &quot;websitetheme.com&quot; or &quot;highdatabase.com&quot; or &quot;incomeupdate.com&quot; or &quot;databasegalore.com&quot; or &quot;panhardware.com&quot; or &quot;zupertech.com&quot; or &quot;freescanonline.com&quot; or &quot;deftsecurity.com&quot; or &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot;) and (dns.question.type:&quot;/[Aa]/&quot; or dns.answer.type:&quot;/[Aa]/&quot;))((dns.question.name:&quot;/.*avsvmcloud\\.com/&quot; or dns.answer.name:&quot;/.*avsvmcloud\\.com/&quot;) and (dns.question.type:&quot;/[Cc][Nn][Aa][Mm][Ee]|[Aa]/&quot; or dns.answer.type:&quot;/[Cc][Nn][Aa][Mm][Ee]|[Aa]/&quot;)) or (dns.question.name:(&quot;deftsecurity.com&quot; or &quot;freescanonline.com&quot; or &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot; or &quot;websitetheme.com&quot; or &quot;highdatabase.com&quot; or &quot;incomeupdate.com&quot; or &quot;databasegalore.com&quot; or &quot;panhardware.com&quot; or &quot;zupertech.com&quot; or &quot;freescanonline.com&quot; or &quot;deftsecurity.com&quot; or &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot;) and (dns.question.type:&quot;/[Aa]/&quot; or dns.answer.type:&quot;/[Aa]/&quot;))</p><h4 id="elastic-dsl" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic DSL</h4><p>{ &quot;bool&quot;: { &quot;should&quot;: [ { &quot;bool&quot;: { &quot;must&quot;: [ { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;dns.question.type&quot;, &quot;dns.answer.type&quot; ], &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/[Cc][Nn][Aa][Mm][Ee]|[Aa]/&quot; } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;dns.question.name&quot;, &quot;dns.answers.name&quot; ], &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*avsvmcloud\\.com/&quot; } } ] } }, { &quot;bool&quot;: { &quot;filter&quot;: [ { &quot;terms&quot;: { &quot;dns.question.name&quot;: [ &quot;deftsecurity.com&quot;, &quot;freescanonline.com&quot;, &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot;, &quot;websitetheme.com&quot;, &quot;highdatabase.com&quot;, &quot;incomeupdate.com&quot;, &quot;databasegalore.com&quot;, &quot;panhardware.com&quot;, &quot;zupertech.com&quot;, &quot;freescanonline.com&quot;, &quot;deftsecurity.com&quot;, &quot;thedoccloud.com&quot; ] } } ], &quot;must&quot;: [ { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;dns.answer.type&quot;, &quot;dns.question.type&quot; ], &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/[Aa]/&quot; } } ] } } ] }<br/>}</p><h3 id="sunburst-suspicious-processes-for-solarwinds-orion-software" class="scroll-m-20 h3">SUNBURST Suspicious Processes for SolarWinds Orion Software</h3><h4 id="splunk" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Splunk</h4><p>index=sysmon sourcetype=&quot;sysmon:xml&quot; ParentImage = &quot;SolarWinds.BusinessLayerHost.exe&quot; AND NOT Image IN (&quot;*\\SolarWinds\\Orion\\APM\\APMServiceControl.exe&quot;, &quot;*\\SolarWinds\\Orion\\ExportToPDFCmd.Exe&quot;,&quot;*\\SolarWinds.Credentials\\SolarWinds.Credentials.Orion.WebApi.exe&quot;, &quot;*\\SolarWinds\\Orion\\Topology\\SolarWinds.Orion.Topology.Calculator.exe&quot;, &quot;*\\SolarWinds\\Orion\\Database-Maint.exe&quot;, &quot;*\\SolarWinds.Orion.ApiPoller.Service\\SolarWinds.Orion.ApiPoller.Service.exe&quot;, &quot;*\\Windows\\SysWOW64\\WerFault.exe&quot;)<br/>| stats values(_time) as occurrences, values(Image) as ChildProcesses, values(CommandLine) as CommandLines count by host, ParentImage<br/>| convert ctime(occurrences)</p><h4 id="elastic-lucene" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic Lucene</h4><p>parent.process.executable:&quot;/.*[Ss][Oo][Ll][Aa][Rr][Ww][Ii][Nn][Dd][Ss]\\.[Bb][Uu][Ss][Ii][Nn][Ee][Ss]+[Ll][Aa][Yy][Ee][Rr][Hh][Oo][Ss][Tt]\\.[Ee][Xx][Ee]/&quot; and not (process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\Windows\\\\SysWOW64\\\\WerFault\\.exe/&quot; OR process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\.Orion\\.ApiPoller\\.Service\\\\SolarWinds\\.Orion\\.ApiPoller\\.Service\\.exe/&quot; or process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\Database-Maint\\.exe/&quot; or process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\Topology\\\\SolarWinds\\.Orion\\.Topology\\.Calculator\\.exe/&quot; or process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\ExportToPDFCmd\\.exe/&quot; or process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\APM\\\\APMServiceControl\\.exe/&quot; or process.executable:&quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds.Credentials\\\\SolarWinds\\.Credentials\\.Orion\\.WebApi\\.exe/&quot;)</p><h4 id="elastic-dsl" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic DSL</h4><p>{ &quot;bool&quot;: { &quot;must&quot;: [ { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*[Ss][Oo][Ll][Aa][Rr][Ww][Ii][Nn][Dd][Ss]\\.[Bb][Uu][Ss][Ii][Nn][Ee][Ss]+[Ll][Aa][Yy][Ee][Rr][Hh][Oo][Ss][Tt]\\.[Ee][Xx][Ee]/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.parent.executable&quot; ] } } ], &quot;must_not&quot;: [ { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\APM\\\\APMServiceControl\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\ExportToPDFCmd\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\Topology\\\\SolarWinds\\.Orion\\.Topology\\.Calculator\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\\\Orion\\\\Database-Maint\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds\\.Orion\\.ApiPoller\\.Service\\\\SolarWinds.Orion\\.ApiPoller\\.Service\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\Windows\\\\SysWOW64\\\\WerFault\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;/.*\\\\SolarWinds.Credentials\\\\SolarWinds\\.Credentials\\.Orion\\.WebApi\\.exe/&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;process.executable&quot; ] } } ] }<br/>}</p><h3 id="sunburst-named-pipe-indicator" class="scroll-m-20 h3">SUNBURST Named Pipe Indicator</h3><h4 id="splunk" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Splunk</h4><p>index=sysmon sourcetype=sysmon:xml (EventID=17 OR EventID=18) PipeName=&quot;583da945-62af-10e8-4902-a8f205c72b2e&quot;<br/>| stats values(_time) as occurrences, values(EventID) as eventID, values(PipeName) as pipeName count by host<br/>| convert ctime(occurrences)</p><h4 id="elastic-lucene" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic Lucene</h4><p>event.code:(&quot;17&quot; or &quot;18&quot;) and file.name:&quot;583da945-62af-10e8-4902-a8f205c72b2e&quot;</p><h4 id="elastic-dsl" class="scroll-m-20 h4">Elastic DSL</h4><p>{ &quot;bool&quot;: { &quot;must&quot;: [ { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;17&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;event.code&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;18&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;event.code&quot; ] } }, { &quot;query_string&quot;: { &quot;query&quot;: &quot;583da945-62af-10e8-4902-a8f205c72b2e&quot;, &quot;fields&quot;: [ &quot;file.name&quot; ] } } ] }<br/>}</p><p>Cyborg Security’s research and development team has built dozens of threat detection packages for the SUNBURST implant and additional malicious behaviours described by FireEye. These packages come tailored to your unique environment and can be immediately downloaded and deployed. You can find these detections, in addition to all known indicators of compromise (IOC), on the HUNTER platform.</p><p>[hubspot type=cta portal=7924572
